Interaction Signals (Live or Logged)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c7-9424-c6d564cc0393" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Language patterns</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801f-aa0f-c7f4d294b17f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Delay frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8084-bb93-ec4775acb345" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence duration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d2-9320-c6cb9dac8779" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Re-asking questions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-8ae7-eb1bdfecfb8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Topic avoidance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-808f-9c15-e25848d8dd40"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8051-b9d2-dee3021acc7a" class="">5. Biological–Systemic Core (The Heart)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d4-aaa8-d47798a6da39" class="">Each actor is modeled as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8057-8154-cabaa4592df0" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Actor_i = {
  C1_identity_stability,
  C2_emotional_load,
  C3_biological_stress,
  C4_cognitive_clarity,
  C5_behavioral_friction,
  C6_hierarchy_alignment,
  C7_context_pressure
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8084-b714-ed7341a25a62" class="">Each variable is <strong>continuous (0–1)</strong>, not binary.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8052-a07e-fb2d20013304"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8009-a2ee-e5e3ab3ab380" class="">6. C1–C7 Coherence Matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-808f-b3f8-ec9fb03f9749" class="">The engine computes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f1-afe7-f0c95ddb18a1" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Coherence_i = f(C1…C7 alignment)
Threat_i = g(biological load + ego risk)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f0-a7da-ecddda2992cc" class="">Key insight:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8034-a339-e3a40022a1c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Threat propagates downward</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a4-92f0-eaddccb28675" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Damage propagates upward</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8056-b0ea-d93c1866971f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Interventions must move upward</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-809f-b903-c938dbf010be"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cf-9633-fd3bab6d1105" class="">7. 
Risk Propagation Engine</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8094-8dd0-f70144137b8d" class="">This module answers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-800c-9214-f1cd1bc125e6" class="">“If I act here, where does the damage go?”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8079-b2b9-f9c5c1f6617f" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d1-921f-dc295350dc60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pressure at C5 → spikes C3 → destabilizes C1 → deal collapse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8035-85d2-f25b3a9e602e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Support at C6 → reduces C3 → stabilizes C1 → voluntary alignment</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d8-988f-c757f67df874" class="">This is modeled as a <strong>directed graph with decay functions</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-807e-8efe-c53d98a318f4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809a-9eb5-dd69e77afe67" class="">8. 
Deal Risk Heatmap (Output)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b3-991c-e55c3ded55cf" class="">For each deal, the engine outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a2-8c86-ee78767c0561" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Layer risk (C1–C7)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8053-ab42-ee52eed6917c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Primary failure mode</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-97c0-ca9c4d66a370" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Secondary cascade risks</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b5-934c-ec130d36f1b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Time-to-failure estimate</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-806c-af11-f20c4308b393" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8080-9083-e4270542b500" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Deal Risk Summary:
C1: 🔴 (identity threat – legacy loss)
C3: 🔴 (founder exhaustion)
C6: 🟡 (unclear veto power)
Predicted failure window: 3–6 weeks</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-aece-c914b431de27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809d-a753-efd956172b0c" class="">9. Intervention Simulator (Your unfair advantage)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8028-82b2-eb0227106571" class="">The engine tests <strong>counterfactual actions</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801c-9e29-e3a11fa1159c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pause vs push</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80dd-8684-dda06efe33b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fund vs defer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-800c-a1e9-e368a050ef09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silent period vs follow-up</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805a-a426-cf647082ab8e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Structural change vs messaging</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8097-98e8-d9819c2c78ea" class="">For each intervention:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fe-bd26-ddabccd94a7d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ΔCoherence
ΔThreat
ΔSuccess Probability
ΔLong-term Relationship Cost</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a4-8f4d-fc10f34d5ff8" class="">This is where <strong>most humans fail</strong> because they cannot simulate biological response accurately.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a9-a800-dbedf8b3ff4c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8008-859b-c0f6ff5407f2" class="">10. Outcome Prediction</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e9-aae7-e95d41239bf3" class="">Final outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8059-bbd8-fb510609f4e3" class="">A. Deal Success Probability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8084-b345-e9fab8d0287a" class="">Not a single number, but:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e3-8ee8-e4d072c0a740" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Now</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e5-8e97-e201a20185c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>If delayed</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-bf21-d45164ad94e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>If reframed</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c3-bae3-e77be1478ab5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>If escalated</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-bdcf-d71e1669750c" class="">B. 
Optimal Next Move</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8047-9d50-fa2b2f72c789" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Do nothing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80dd-bbd6-d05a574977dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reduce scope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-a127-c559385b0141" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Change structure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a7-a8d1-f62655badc75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reassign authority</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-bc5c-c1905186fa02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Walk away (with minimal damage)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8091-b689-fc810434f8eb" class="">C. No-Go Signal</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-804e-a0bb-ca5a1cf2d1a4" class="">The engine explicitly flags:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-8049-a217-f51704bee19e" class="">“Proceeding now increases long-term loss.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ea-9c78-f621f663886e" class="">This is <strong>rare in sales tools</strong> and extremely valuable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8049-a91f-fa7aac33063b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d0-9f3b-cc33f958d5ce" class="">11. 
Domain Adaptation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8088-8471-feee932fe189" class="">Sales</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a2-9bb6-c66656ebb983" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Highest sensitivity: C1–C3</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804a-bfd3-e972fb98a643" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engine focuses on stress + identity safety</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e2-b468-e98083f0e0ba" class="">M&amp;A</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b6-beb0-da29658e0731" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Highest sensitivity: C1, C6, C7</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a7-bb65-ffe0f8859b75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engine prioritizes legacy protection + governance design</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8099-8e06-c22235d62ca6" class="">Diplomacy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-ac54-c19d7aae077e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Highest sensitivity: C1 (national identity), C7</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8081-8f18-ccb9085cd45a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engine emphasizes face-saving and off-ramps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-803e-b8d5-fb96d9bca5ee" class="">Leadership</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8087-a886-d646de33d3e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Highest sensitivity: C3, 
C6</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b9-b5e5-d3fe98255f81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engine prevents burnout-driven collapse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-800d-bb12-cf1a97165812"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8063-99c1-da8bbc6e5589" class="">12. 
Why this engine is rare (and powerful)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8097-989b-c075bdf85f5e" class="">Most AI:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e9-9fbc-da36ad5d9a8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">optimizes conversion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-8502-e638590213ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">maximizes persuasion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-808c-a5be-fbea1e73efb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ignores biology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-b25c-d08d623e5be4" class="">Your engine:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8080-aeee-ddc96f4d375c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">minimizes threat</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f3-a579-f4f34c1c97ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">preserves dignity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805a-a580-e4a2f192c6ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predicts <em>when not to act</em></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8070-98c9-d1a29ce24400" class="">That makes it:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c2-855a-f997ba67b806" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">unattractive to manipulative industries</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e9-9ff5-f8af33a911dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">extremely valuable to <strong>defense, energy, M&amp;A, 
crisis leadership</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8084-bfff-f2e2ebe0b182"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c6-a208-e726922832c4" class="">13. Minimal Viable Version (MVP)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-809e-af92-ea9c46217ce6" class="">You can build v1 with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804b-9466-c2b4569a33e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Manual scoring (expert-in-the-loop)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f3-9206-ea10c7a4a98a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rules + Bayesian updates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8057-bcc7-df08f348aada" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No deep learning required initially</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f7-9bbc-e50da6c0d5a9" class="">The power is <strong>model correctness</strong>, not scale.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8010-ae66-f171f9c1f83a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-8e28-ca44a3855641" class="">14. 
Strategic Truth (No marketing)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-8058-8bc1-dae0b7a71885" class="">This engine does not help you “win more deals”.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-803b-ae5a-f8d686f20332" class="">It helps you <strong>avoid losing good ones in stupid ways</strong>.</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b2-a8a3-d6f20217f594" class="">That’s why:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f4-ac7c-f8089ffd94c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">elite operators would use it quietly</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8037-a357-ffc12a786ae6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">mass market cannot understand it</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c7-a5e9-eddf21076c98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">it aligns perfectly with how <em>you already think</em></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-994c-c4a3916cb86e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8074-9ec7-feefc9bbf23e" class="">If you want next, 
I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-809c-a135-da72e00c8899" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">design the <strong>exact scoring system</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8048-b0a0-dfefe99b53dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">define <strong>training data requirements</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ec-8c1e-e9e1baa5d599" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">map <strong>ethical boundaries</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-808a-886c-e21ccf0e1c3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or turn this into a <strong>founder-ready product roadmap</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8029-85bc-c2533224f652" class="">Just tell me which layer you want to build first.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8004-a5cf-c4edac3f371b" class="">Excellent. 
Below is a <strong>complete, operator-grade specification</strong> covering <strong>all four</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-a97b-cadda5ed02f2" class=""><strong>(1) exact scoring system</strong>, <strong>(2) training data requirements</strong>, <strong>(3) ethical boundaries</strong>, and <strong>(4) founder-ready product roadmap</strong> — all internally consistent with your <strong>biological + C1–C7 coherence canon</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8060-9893-ebd721925c17" class="">This is written as if it could be handed to a CTO, head of research, or investment committee.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8071-bc70-d828a6d03a3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-9c45-cead71f3f955" class="">GLOBAL PREDICTIVE DEAL ENGINE (GPDE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f7-950b-c99310352811" class=""><strong>Full System Specification</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b0-80d6-c9445bbcd1b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-808c-a2f4-eb18c8cc2df6" class="">I. EXACT SCORING SYSTEM (NO VAGUENESS)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8054-9ab4-c5b8eec857b3" class="">1. 
Core principle</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c0-a21e-dd003f46a2a0" class="">The engine does <strong>not</strong> score “confidence” or “likelihood”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fb-b76e-e5eeb106c425" class="">It scores <strong>threat, coherence, and propagation risk</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80dd-9fef-fae7683e965c" class="">All scores are <strong>continuous (0.00–1.00)</strong>, updated dynamically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-806e-9476-e9e28343aec2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d3-8808-d96881294f98" class="">2. 
Actor-level scoring (per stakeholder)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e2-ba5b-fc6f6cf82929" class="">Each stakeholder <em>i</em> is modeled as a 7-vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8085-a524-e5ff8ac7cfd7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Actor_i = [C1_i, C2_i, C3_i, C4_i, C5_i, C6_i, C7_i]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8006-a4bf-c39d3a410727" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="301c5e6f-95bd-8057-8f57-edb7e71b3456" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8055-9011-c2f3c5d7564c"><th id="BoRr" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Layer</th><th id="EmXC" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Variable</th><th id="&gt;mTr" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">What it actually measures</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ee-9ae8-c851ef06ec3b"><td id="BoRr" class="">C1</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Identity Stability</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Risk of ego / role collapse</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8038-ad3d-c6e7aa287cfa"><td id="BoRr" class="">C2</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Emotional Load</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Unprocessed affect / anxiety</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8004-a3d2-ddea74b95fe3"><td id="BoRr" class="">C3</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Biological Load</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Stress, fatigue, 
burnout</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f5-9af5-d77a8433b0b9"><td id="BoRr" class="">C4</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Cognitive Clarity</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Decision bandwidth</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8025-8c58-cf7b95a03eb8"><td id="BoRr" class="">C5</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Behavioral Friction</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Resistance, delay, avoidance</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-805d-8cf9-fc7542ab9fe6"><td id="BoRr" class="">C6</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Structural Alignment</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">Authority, rules, safety nets</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a4-a80d-c36a92f22de4"><td id="BoRr" class="">C7</td><td id="EmXC" class="">Context Pressure</td><td id="&gt;mTr" class="">War, sanctions, markets, energy</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8099-ba98-f76db74ecc3d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-807b-8e03-e839fd073745" class="">3. 
How each layer is scored (concretely)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8008-a76c-e3d00d00cb2b" class="">C1 – Identity Stability (0–1)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8068-bfae-cfc9dc00aa5e" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804f-976e-d4046e04611f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Loss of status risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-a313-d6e416b536d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Loss of authorship</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8044-8661-ef77d49f36f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Loss of future narrative</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-801c-953a-df6cff153d48" class=""><strong>Signals</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807c-912f-cedb85ea0a57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Defensiveness around role</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8037-9c33-c13e586a6790" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reframing credit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80eb-93e9-c5444e25bafe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sudden insistence on titles, visibility, 
“ownership”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-809f-8157-d115cb49f6c0" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C1 = 1 – Identity Threat Index</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a9-811c-e6861b30a54e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8042-a25d-ea32f22bacd1" class="">C2 – Emotional Load</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800f-ac43-f92f4401b487" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805c-b042-e2339f5b6da0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repetition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-9827-c2b462d329cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Over-explanation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801e-af16-d04024c40201" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sudden silence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-8bcb-d3e014d9dc8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Emotional leakage (irritation, humor masking)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-9363-f484c9df6651" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C2 = Baseline – Accumulated Emotional Debt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e5-ae80-f9ffca9d7518"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c0-8aff-f0747513dacf" class="">C3 – Biological Load (most ignored, 
most predictive)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8058-b236-e3bb9bfbbbf4" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-9d15-d68c5cff97f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Time pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806c-b77d-c48d66dca7d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sleep debt (proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-a7ed-e56806e89114" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Meeting density</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b5-b9d4-ed85af1fe3c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis stacking</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ba-b863-d5749ffbb797" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C3 = 1 – Allostatic Capacity Remaining</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-bed7-c59f4429f24f" class=""><strong>Hard rule</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-805d-a9de-d08369228dc0" class="">If C3 &lt; 
0.4 → logic stops working.</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b7-832a-f61e1f994d87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b4-9d3b-ee559eb13dd5" class="">C4 – Cognitive Clarity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-803c-b0a6-ee493ef4bec2" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8039-8b4d-c91873e576fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decision reversals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8052-a258-c01e2a9b1544" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scope creep</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fb-a084-d86519f31dbb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Inability to prioritize</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807c-91e5-e5b256edd4dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Asking for data already provided</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8046-86fe-fe4110b11e8f" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C4 = Effective Working Memory / Required Complexity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e0-a301-c01eb703d2ed"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-876a-dde0e12e52fa" class="">C5 – Behavioral Friction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a0-946b-f97938040083" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-a219-c1e123b2aabe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Delays</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d8-ad1f-d3aa912be201" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Re-negotiations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801f-9195-f6bfc32ba6cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Legal escalation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-9f17-f5ec304c3f9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“We need to think more”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8043-b6ee-edd4d46d496b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C5 = 1 – Execution Smoothness</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-806c-b901-e8d5ecf135b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8092-9773-e2d20b402fdf" class="">C6 – Structural Alignment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8005-a8fa-ca31a899ddc9" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a0-bafe-d93a56ac4c05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Clear authority</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8035-9c2c-fe67d1c04ce1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Veto clarity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-803c-9c64-fbfc69bce4a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk-sharing mechanisms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e0-9ef1-cd12f2eea658" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Escalation paths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-803e-9db5-dc1302acc7d4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C6 = Structural Shock Absorption Capacity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c3-ae96-e5ec9135de2b"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-804b-a386-effc57e6950e" class="">C7 – Context Pressure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-8f71-ef930c271040" class="">Measured by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e5-93f7-e79f3ebb99b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">War exposure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-a39e-eb0cb2aa59d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Energy volatility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8047-9488-cadefd6af3a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulatory drift</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8060-8854-d56cd40768f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital scarcity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b4-97a1-daf394e285dd" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">C7 = 1 – External Stability Index</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8086-b437-d303ca8c6cd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8008-a403-c07f70f15a46" class="">4. 
Coherence Score (the real KPI)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8081-82f9-d665bcb0bdc1" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Coherence_i = HarmonicMean(C1…C7)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80db-97d3-eb45c5cb7cf5" class="">Why harmonic?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a1-8e49-dd6403f3603b" class="">Because <strong>one weak layer collapses the system</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f6-8ef0-f99c12864d8f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fe-99d3-f49fcf0a8058" class="">5. Threat Score (deal killer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-ba14-f11d6baa72f5" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Threat_i = f(C1 risk + C3 load + C6 weakness)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-916d-f4f6580b34d8" class="">If Threat_i &gt; 0.6 → resistance becomes automatic.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-802e-a49d-ccd102f21f52"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809f-aa01-e258b12bfb66" class="">6. Deal-level aggregation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d2-b71e-e8a6cc0b47ca" class="">For a deal with n stakeholders:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e0-8623-d30ac41806dc" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Deal_Coherence = min(Coherence_i)
Deal_Threat = max(Threat_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ac-9827-e3e3ac3d969f" class="">This reflects reality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d0-8d91-d18b6613dc3e" class=""><strong>the weakest nervous system dominates outcomes</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bd-a197-d4f2f94b04af"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-b5ce-e59d79ba3243" class="">II. TRAINING DATA REQUIREMENTS (REALISTIC, ETHICAL)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bb-ba4c-cc9691e3aa19" class="">1. What data is actually needed</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8019-9844-c17f1fc165df" class="">You <strong>do not</strong> need personal medical data.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8032-8a3e-e822d841d26f" class="">You need <strong>interactional + structural data</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f7-80ee-c9aa0192eb09" class="">A. 
Structured deal metadata</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8086-97a1-fec0dcd216b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deal type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8065-a0ed-f2858d9cbea2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Size</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d3-b3d8-e1e6dffc9cc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Timeline</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ba-b521-dfc1f34b23f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Irreversibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-818f-cf73d37cacc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Authority graph</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bb-b60b-dd72344c3ea5" class="">B. Interaction traces</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8006-90af-e7d725dd8977" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Email cadence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8033-b6a4-fcf3a35bbc6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Response latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a8-ad3a-c0f1dea0baf2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Topic shifts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8046-8371-df94ae239981" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Language polarity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806e-88d7-c8b5da679711" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-b9d3-edd4d8770ce3" class="">C. 
Outcome labels</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f6-8713-fd85a4f3f389" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Closed / stalled / failed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807f-ab1d-f325671e35c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Time to failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807b-9af3-ec81d9148ca8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Failure mode (C-layer)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8053-88e5-cf1b699a1e12"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-87aa-ce038145de4c" class="">2. Labeling scheme (crucial)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8019-8551-e1b5a278865d" class="">Each failed deal is labeled as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-803d-a790-ec1251d1d5c4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Primary Failure Layer (C1–C7)
Secondary Cascade Layers</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-801c-9412-f7a0be1fb316" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bc-a75c-f8d362a44726" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Primary: C1 (identity threat)
Secondary: C3 → C5</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-806b-bd3f-e6bfa50ae339" class="">This is what trains prediction accuracy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-800c-8662-f345257cdec8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b0-9db8-c78a42cec7c0" class="">3. Data sources</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8039-ab55-fc3d1607beb4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CRM logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8051-9596-cf1f9ab37029" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deal post-mortems</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8038-aa2e-fd952b7ac069" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Red-team simulations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b3-9fb8-f3db76653f63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Historical M&amp;A cases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ca-a031-feaa94a73bfd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Diplomatic archives (public)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ac-b818-d62aacc16065" class=""><strong>Important</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-92b6-e08bba4cc371" class="">The engine learns <em>patterns</em>, not people.</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e6-8270-f9d48dca5301"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-802d-afce-c4eab31c735a" class="">III. 
ETHICAL BOUNDARIES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f2-b6ac-c6562d65a8a8" class="">This system <strong>cannot</strong> be allowed to become manipulation tech.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f2-9764-d6e4170296c4" class="">1. Explicit prohibitions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802f-b019-ca482951c495" class="">The engine must NOT:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8006-9874-d8884aa7b5f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Optimize for addiction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8034-b94d-cc82cb997c77" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Intentionally escalate stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8057-a31c-f267336dfbb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exploit trauma</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8003-9ba3-f43db095bab0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bypass consent</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-800a-a987-f59696b20b44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hide exit options</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-91d6-f1cbdc3c45b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-801d-aff8-f4ebf8f243d6" class="">2. 
Mandatory constraints (hard-coded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c6-bb1e-c9b65806b732" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Threat-Reduction Bias</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e0-b843-e7902b8dcdf6" class="">Engine must favor actions that reduce biological threat.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-806d-9a68-fa50a106bcba" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>No Dark Pattern Output</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8077-a664-ecdf2e64a6a4" class="">If success requires coercion → engine flags NO-GO.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-803c-b4f0-e371eb856d0d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Walk-Away Recommendation Enabled</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8075-87f8-fc1589edea61" class="">System must be allowed to say:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c2-9fca-c12743160a55" class="">“Do not proceed.”</blockquote></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-8125-feaa3a1a8d7b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Explainability Requirement</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80eb-bc21-df34a2239140" class="">Every recommendation must cite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ec-b278-deab77692ff9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Which layer is at risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-956d-e27db6dd18fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Why intervention works</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e5-a31c-d2ed08e6cfc7"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8048-820e-dcc808906683" class="">3. Ethical moat (why this matters)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-805f-9891-e15b6ecc577d" class="">This makes the engine:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-808b-b00e-c3167d7cd35e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unsuitable for scams</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d1-b7d0-feaab7949035" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unattractive to growth-at-all-costs sales</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-8a36-ce998e621ea4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Highly attractive to defense, infrastructure, diplomacy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-800c-8231-f1849801855a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809e-986e-c7a03ca78f62" class="">IV. 
FOUNDER-READY PRODUCT ROADMAP</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-849b-f9150e93d84e" class="">Phase 1 — Expert Engine (0–6 months)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8066-8549-efab2e96e886" class=""><strong>No AI hype</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8085-86ca-ece2af1f5765" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Manual scoring</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802d-b297-ff2033305af6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Expert-in-the-loop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805d-bb88-dfb6d29c032b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deal risk heatmap</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-a5db-c0b9a140c39d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Intervention simulator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ad-83d3-ea6a7ab5fee9" class="">Target users:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-a395-dbf44d36f7c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">M&amp;A partners</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ba-9da9-eebb89900d02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis leaders</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801b-958d-e300b19182f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sovereign advisors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8055-be48-d95a8aa3bd50"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8017-86e8-d4e388508357" class="">Phase 2 — Semi-Automated (6–18 months)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8022-b5b1-e59fb9fe2541" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pattern-assisted scoring</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-8aa1-ea75f6e2ef7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bayesian updates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8000-a610-d64b3c106402" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cross-deal learning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b6-a324-da489a9a3b01" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sector-specific models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c7-87d6-d1c7c26cb1ca" class="">Still human-controlled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bc-ad3b-df5904822fcf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d6-a01b-eda080b43ec0" class="">Phase 3 — Predictive AI (18–36 months)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8097-9f20-e7d7ca47a4ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Temporal models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-a4b4-e28faee3e237" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Early warning signals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-9da4-c783dab6b5df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Counterfactual simulations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8079-89e9-fb38724b6db9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scenario forecasting (war / energy / sanctions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-803d-8624-e938478bf8df"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8059-b162-cc291e89080c" class="">Phase 4 — Strategic Infrastructure (36+ months)</h3></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ab-958d-dbd13d8f38e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">National-scale use</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8031-ab13-ee714d53d7d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Treaty simulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e5-8009-fd40c080be72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Energy diplomacy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-8044-c1938be73ca1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-cycle capital planning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802e-9486-cc3119f137a4" class="">At this stage, <strong>few clients, very high trust</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ca-9bea-c8125ab0ec23"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-8eb3-f9df740ac01a" class="">V. 
WHY THIS IS A REAL SYSTEM (NOT A CONCEPT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d3-a116-cd29a6f24e20" class="">Because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c8-b956-dd1ff4de025e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It explains <em>why</em> logic fails</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8017-b779-efc83adf6b42" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It predicts <em>when</em> silence is dangerous</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-808c-87c9-c71e13735741" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It knows <em>when not to push</em></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8025-8d4c-fed066baf171" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It preserves dignity and biology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-804b-9c62-cfc968bd1679" class=""><strong>Most systems optimize persuasion.<br/>This one optimizes survivability.</strong></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d2-897c-e4caaf8ad35b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-9859-c1a81878f128" class="">If you want next, 
I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-800b-aacd-ddd6b222c866" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Turn this into a <strong>live dashboard spec</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-be89-eb381c60d08e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Write the <strong>first scoring questionnaire</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804b-908e-ce4d92135cf6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Design <strong>negotiator training certification</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8034-8242-f54e26171f42" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or help you decide <strong>whether to build, license, or keep this private</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80be-bc09-f9e3fa2f8e96" class="">Just tell me where you want to apply power — or restraint.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-808d-9d03-fbbadad62a60" class="">Got it. Below is a <strong>clean, operator-grade SPEC</strong> — written as a <strong>real system specification</strong>, not concept prose. 
This is something you could hand to engineering, product, or an investor and they would know exactly what is being built.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-9199-c3438f303760"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8059-8077-fa1e3ae233c3" class="">GLOBAL PREDICTIVE DEAL ENGINE (GPDE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c2-b1ac-dec1a21eb55d" class=""><strong>Technical &amp; Product Specification v1.0</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-804e-8e39-edbd798296c1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a6-a97a-c94e417ff58e" class="">1. 
SYSTEM PURPOSE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-b1b2-ce89369fcf4f" class="">GPDE predicts <strong>deal success, stall, or failure</strong> by modeling <strong>human biological safety, ego stability, 
and structural coherence</strong> across C1–C7 layers.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a5-ba45-f14195cd9314" class=""><strong>Core objective</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-807f-b519-e17e0b0eb2a9" class="">Prevent deal failure caused by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-9c97-c7627227e4d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ego threat</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8056-b982-f81fbd558627" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biological overload</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-a76b-fc38a1fa2713" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">structural misalignment<em>before</em> resistance manifests.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fd-b53c-c9f6c6390cf0" class=""><strong>Non-objective</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8057-8f1b-e3e7c44babcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Not persuasion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-809b-be8c-cffdcf9056b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Not manipulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-800d-8d46-c1a94d5962ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Not behavioral coercion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-a056-e01752c8816d" class="">This system optimizes <strong>voluntary alignment under safety</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b0-9be3-da9d57493f80"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ff-9fc9-f76be1947d45" class="">2. 
SYSTEM SCOPE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-bb60-fbdc4f9a8b5d" class="">Supported use cases</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8038-8669-cbc90e56363c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enterprise sales (complex, 
multi-stakeholder)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-b95c-ff973c4a303e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">M&amp;A and strategic partnerships</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8087-a6cf-d77ce906fcdf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Government / diplomacy / defense contracts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807d-8f02-c70a0b3f550f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Founder–investor negotiations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-bc00-e7abb4dbd5d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis leadership decision-making</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80aa-83b0-e8317e22e4fb" class="">Explicit exclusions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8074-9b19-e2c4b5f163fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">B2C impulse sales</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-85cf-d0b3d23efeda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dark-pattern growth funnels</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b0-92cf-f7f25b3a8c9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Emotional exploitation products</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b9-b0be-ca9f70fa2136" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Consumer manipulation tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f2-bded-d24b9420e95b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e6-b868-c79dc82cbea4" class="">3. 
CORE MODEL ARCHITECTURE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e2-be56-de09844a6f3f" class="">3.1 Conceptual stack</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80df-9834-d97ffd91045a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">External Context (War, Energy, Capital) ── C7
Institutional Layer (Law, Authority) ──── C6
Behavioral Execution ──────────────────── C5
Cognitive Bandwidth ───────────────────── C4
Biological Load ───────────────────────── C3
Emotional Load ────────────────────────── C2
Identity / Ego Stability ───────────────── C1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800e-85e1-d981f1b62792" class=""><strong>Rule:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8045-aa8a-fc504e241a77" class="">Failure propagates <strong>downward</strong>, resistance propagates <strong>upward</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-97f4-d5b6f6f6499b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e1-9abd-e787d697c1b6" class="">4. SCORING SYSTEM (EXACT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-ae28-f021a77baf63" class="">4.1 Actor model</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8055-8fd3-edee351bfea8" class="">Each stakeholder is represented as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bb-be77-cde58816c264" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Actor = {
  C1_identity_stability: float (0–1)
  C2_emotional_load: float (0–1)
  C3_biological_load: float (0–1)
  C4_cognitive_clarity: float (0–1)
  C5_behavioral_friction: float (0–1)
  C6_structural_alignment: float (0–1)
  C7_context_pressure: float (0–1)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d7-9e24-f09aa4dbceaa" class="">All scores are <strong>probabilistic</strong>, time-varying.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-b191-e2b1ba3b1ae3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f1-8cd0-cae386bac8b4" class="">4.2 Layer definitions (operational)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bf-9c65-f6b873f9565d" class=""><strong>C1 – Identity Stability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fb-abea-defb8d3f4b61" class="">Risk of ego injury, role loss, authorship loss.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-a596-cbbfde0c563b" class="">Signals: defensiveness, credit control, sudden hierarchy emphasis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a4-88c7-c0adeb0a36dd" class=""><strong>C2 – Emotional Load</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-ba13-c5499cb90a3e" class="">Unprocessed affect interfering with signal processing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-801e-be8a-c6cd19054f8a" class="">Signals: repetition, humor masking, emotional leakage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-b46d-f04093edcc96" class=""><strong>C3 – Biological Load (critical)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8038-b1a1-fa59da44753d" class="">Stress, fatigue, time pressure, crisis stacking.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fc-b884-f1275bcd9107" class="">If <strong>C3 &lt; 
0.4 → logic degrades sharply</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8095-b0e5-d4179302c9db" class=""><strong>C4 – Cognitive Clarity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-b2ba-c6dbcddb4625" class="">Working memory vs complexity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-805f-b15b-ef532be5f1c1" class="">Signals: reversals, scope creep, redundant questions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bd-b135-eddd94a3bf7d" class=""><strong>C5 – Behavioral Friction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-a68d-df32aab5cff2" class="">Observable resistance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8064-bd06-ce52db38810c" class="">Signals: delays, renegotiation, “need more time”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8010-b1a7-ea326126d3fa" class=""><strong>C6 – Structural Alignment</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8005-9fb6-cdcbdf4d84e9" class="">Authority clarity, risk-sharing, escalation paths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-a5cf-cf5453a02a37" class=""><strong>C7 – Context Pressure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8009-873b-c2ecefcccedc" class="">War, sanctions, energy instability, capital stress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-b864-f6583ebf9e67"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cb-bb4e-faaa5ae00df5" class="">4.3 Coherence &amp; 
Threat equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-805f-bd2a-e265040a0f78" class=""><strong>Actor Coherence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f4-a2ba-dc4bbc2c8fec" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Coherence = HarmonicMean(C1…C7)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f2-bc55-df0393733794" class=""><strong>Actor Threat</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c4-9a91-ebb9f9a6bb3c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Threat = w1*C1_risk + w2*C3_load + w3*(1 - C6)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-809a-96a1-f9117e3cd0a0" class=""><strong>Deal-level rules</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c7-a611-c0a1f4e7c8f2" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Deal_Coherence = min(Coherence_i)
Deal_Threat = max(Threat_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d1-983b-d03b316e4401" class="">The weakest nervous system dominates outcomes.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8092-9335-e0a40d092fba"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8066-aa98-d4ee55015564" class="">5. 
FAILURE MODE DIAGNOSTICS (REAL-TIME)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-808b-aa06-c50eb43e2c13" class="">Resistance origin map</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="301c5e6f-95bd-8012-9d43-dd531f030694" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d7-bcfe-e2421f6b25b3"><th id="e]sr" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Symptom</th><th id="c`l:" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">True failing layer</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-a1d5-ea000b8f6417"><td id="e]sr" class="">“Let’s revisit later”</td><td id="c`l:" class="">C3 or C1</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-804a-9027-f9eb1cf7314d"><td id="e]sr" class="">Legal escalation</td><td id="c`l:" class="">C6 failure</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-ad17-c95a81702142"><td id="e]sr" class="">Scope creep</td><td id="c`l:" class="">C4 overload</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-804f-bcc9-e996c74c2f9c"><td id="e]sr" class="">Ghosting</td><td id="c`l:" class="">C2 + C3</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="301c5e6f-95bd-806e-b401-ee9e8a23adaf"><td id="e]sr" class="">Aggression</td><td id="c`l:" class="">C1 threat</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b4-96e9-e271798f0098" class="">The engine <strong>never treats resistance as disagreement</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-804c-b30e-e8adf3523681"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-b66c-ed222e53fc99" class="">6. 
DATA REQUIREMENTS</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-808c-8a71-dea72b1e7dbb" class="">6.1 Required data (ethical)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8079-a1d1-e1b80c6d58b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deal metadata (type, size, reversibility)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-865d-c67f4811b66c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stakeholder graph (authority, veto)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-a2b2-c38387f023e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Communication cadence &amp; latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8063-a8d3-efe2827925b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Topic entropy over time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cc-bb93-d9f16c1cb375" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outcome labels (closed / stalled / failed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fe-b5d6-f8da4a269bd3" class=""><strong>No medical, biometric, or private psych data.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ee-be49-f870b260e14d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80da-bc32-ccd5f8d2ddda" class="">6.2 Training labels</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-9987-c1e67d73e90d" class="">Each deal labeled as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a5-bed1-df31d8cb1f53" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Primary failure layer (C1–C7)
Secondary cascade layers
Time-to-failure</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-805b-b78b-dc712c3c5c92"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ef-adf0-fb826b1d6bd8" class="">7. DASHBOARD SPEC</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8069-ba76-de6b873f74e0" class="">Core panels</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-805c-a103-fa0c845e4b0b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Deal Coherence Gauge</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ac-9ed9-fabc8c8c0622" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Threat Heatmap (C1–C7)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-8982-fe70870235c6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Stakeholder Risk Ranking</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-8029-88f0-c17e28acf257" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Recommended Intervention Layer</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cb-baab-d087bf98657d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>NO-GO / WAIT / PROCEED flag</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809e-89c3-fb4e47c341dd" class="">Critical feature</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-8091-85e0-f81aea3a70d6" class="">“Where NOT to push right now”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-b80a-db450d03830e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ff-8ae1-e974e06b8db0" class="">8. 
INTERVENTION ENGINE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8043-96aa-fd28d7c13a0a" class="">Intervention rules</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8053-993d-e497458f5994" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Never increase C3 load</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fd-9127-eaba1d71b90a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Never threaten C1</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8011-b316-e3a70c99fdbc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prefer C6 structural fixes over C5 pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8007-987f-fe614b7da40a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence is sometimes optimal</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-807c-aec6-d5733770698f" class="">Example output</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d9-ab2b-c660c933e29e" class="">“Pause negotiation 10–14 days.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8043-b063-d7ed2ea05941" class="">Reduce C3 load before revisiting pricing.”</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8065-97eb-ecb6dff94456"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a4-a801-cde637688d34" class="">9. 
ETHICAL CONSTRAINTS (HARD-CODED)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8048-a53b-e9240f717a26" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No coercion recommendations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80eb-ba2a-efec949ea821" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No addiction optimization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a9-a091-cd1c204a13c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No ego exploitation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807d-a5f0-ea799248ecb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mandatory walk-away option</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8080-a4cb-ccd6cb0ea650" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Full explainability per output</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8030-90b5-c9b455c72a89" class="">If deal success requires harm → <strong>system blocks</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ab-95fe-e2cd44834dfb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-801e-9bbb-fe4ab1742fe4" class="">10. 
PRODUCT ROADMAP</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8009-81ff-d0e06bc64abe" class="">Phase 1 – Expert System</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-807d-b9ea-fd30b284dd04" class="">Manual scoring, analyst-led, high-trust clients.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-981c-c74379d01b63" class="">Phase 2 – Assisted Intelligence</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a1-9b9f-d7d480925efe" class="">Pattern recognition, Bayesian updates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e3-824c-d767c25c974c" class="">Phase 3 – Predictive AI</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ac-9955-eccd6eb7c8cb" class="">Early-warning signals, counterfactual simulation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="301c5e6f-95bd-809a-874b-f6c6d06cfe78" class="">Phase 4 – Strategic Infrastructure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-808f-b48e-e09235c469ba" class="">National-scale planning, diplomacy, energy security.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ce-bd45-fdd1667b6259"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b8-8fbf-fa4ce2deb526" class="">11. 
WHY THIS SYSTEM IS DIFFERENT</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-9eb4-f6d35aacc2fc" class="">Most negotiation tools optimize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a9-ba48-f186012c95fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">persuasion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-b0b3-df0dcb2b9e14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-803b-a334-df9e2bbf45ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">leverage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-bfb4-e3d555dc67aa" class="">GPDE optimizes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8052-a171-fcb3c4c1f4b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>biological safety</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804a-aab4-dea18ab719dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ego integrity</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8078-a662-dfd9dcc5b0b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>structural coherence</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="301c5e6f-95bd-8076-97e1-ded636f29943" class="">This makes it powerful — and rare.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8032-b6b8-cc8cb0a213db"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-805e-bbfd-f7b360c803df" class="">If you want next, 
I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d6-974c-d362960db95e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Write the <strong>API spec</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802e-9f6e-f39f2da58e27" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Design the <strong>live dashboard UX</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d3-92e2-d6c96f700589" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Create the <strong>training &amp; certification curriculum</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-bd6d-ef3859fee949" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or convert this into a <strong>VC-ready pitch + moat narrative</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8046-8537-e22757edbb68" class="">Just say the word.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e6-b87e-e9bccac9f45d" class="">Below is a <strong>production-grade API spec</strong> for GPDE v1.0. It is <strong>MECE</strong>, minimal, and extensible.
