This is written so engineering can implement immediately.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e3-afc3-e356054b06a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8026-a37e-fe1a263f1887" class="">GPDE API v1.0 — REST + Webhooks</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-a731-cd6fbc10e57e" class="">0) Conventions</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8065-aeda-f33c279996f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Base URL</strong>: <code>https://api.gpde.ai/v1</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807e-bb4e-ff961811deee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Auth</strong>: <code>Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8009-b8ad-c6d470e7eada" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Content-Type</strong>: <code>application/json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c6-9c61-ffd5b5ee5726" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Idempotency</strong>: <code>Idempotency-Key: &lt;uuid&gt;</code> on write endpoints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fe-90c3-cac2c399faa3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Time</strong>: ISO-8601 UTC</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8067-9055-e7adbc055b45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Scores</strong>: floats <strong>0.0–1.0</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8091-861e-cd1144eaf30e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Enums</strong>: strict; 
unknown values rejected</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8029-8803-deb0d753f7cc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8082-bca1-ca2cab0be541" class="">1) Data Model</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80da-8a7a-e2bb622f0941" class="">1.1 Deal</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a7-bb42-c67bfe15efb7" class="">A “deal” is the container for all context, actors, interactions, scoring snapshots, and recommendations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8086-8f17-ca331c36f134" class=""><strong>Core fields</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8094-8d32-f821aa0e2fb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deal_id</code> (string, UUID)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807c-b1a7-e37845265a61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deal_type</code> (enum: <code>sales</code>, <code>mna</code>, <code>partnership</code>, <code>diplomacy</code>, <code>leadership</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-8108-ec87e9d9adf9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stage</code> (enum: <code>prospecting</code>, <code>discovery</code>, <code>proposal</code>, <code>negotiation</code>, <code>diligence</code>, <code>closing</code>, <code>post_close</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8001-a583-ca9cb1e53d87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>currency</code> (ISO 4217)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-9126-eea818d1a06a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>size</code> (number, 
optional)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-808f-9a88-e103230de2db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>reversibility</code> (enum: <code>high</code>, <code>medium</code>, <code>low</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805b-8806-f2452003b73b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>time_horizon_days</code> (int)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c5-b631-cb608f860d39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>context</code> (object: C7 inputs)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8094-9843-c04f2e10b7bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>actors</code> (array of Actor refs)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f8-9332-dffb2d59978b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stakeholder_graph</code> (object)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c0-a7e3-e6f7c5657952" class="">1.2 Actor (stakeholder)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-91e3-deb8eaf95295" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>actor_id</code> (UUID)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8037-b71e-f1bc73c96cb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>type</code> (enum: <code>individual</code>, <code>organization</code>, 
<code>state</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e8-a024-efa8ad36cc8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>role</code> (string)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-b09d-ccb04d5c3ff4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>authority</code> (enum: <code>decision</code>, <code>veto</code>, <code>influencer</code>, <code>operator</code>, <code>unknown</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a7-a893-f1c0a382467e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>alignment</code> (enum: <code>supportive</code>, <code>neutral</code>, <code>resistant</code>, <code>unknown</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8056-aa48-c20d61d1f8d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>notes</code> (string, optional)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f3-bf3a-f34ee1bf48e6" class="">1.3 Interaction event</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8003-bfe7-f02514ca5933" class="">Used for real-time diagnosis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ce-b3ac-cb59e5269550" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>event_id</code> (UUID)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801e-8d2b-c7416e4fe77c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>channel</code> (enum: <code>email</code>, <code>chat</code>, <code>call</code>, <code>meeting</code>, 
<code>doc</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d2-b9b9-ef0a5da96199" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8066-9a2b-db7bede26bc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>from_actor_id</code>, <code>to_actor_ids</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ee-9394-cfe492b362ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>content</code> (string for text; 
transcript for call/meeting)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8046-9847-d466bb5c594f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metadata</code> (latency_ms, duration_sec, attachments, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8062-a9d3-ca84e9e01208" class="">1.4 Score snapshot</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806d-9395-c827d5a57928" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>snapshot_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8061-8452-ee28453c63bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8049-8294-d44bfcce8823" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scope</code> (enum: <code>deal</code>, 
<code>actor</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8037-a50c-d638c6387969" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>actor_id</code> (optional)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805d-9f9a-d67b88fed145" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scores</code>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-bf9f-c6993c29681f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c1_identity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cc-accb-dbfb7869c0f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c2_emotion</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ad-8ecd-f1f2c1472975" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c3_biology</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8015-b3cb-f959b02ce403" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c4_cognition</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8092-add7-d30300bfa9ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c5_behavior</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8089-98cd-d3530d4d4041" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c6_structure</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d0-887b-eb28421648d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>c7_context</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a6-bb4d-f6d075177784" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>coherence</code> (harmonic mean)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8012-956b-daf23c478e28" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>threat</code> (weighted)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8002-8faa-d893383e6e2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>dci</code> (product or harmonic; 
configurable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f5-918b-cbbe6a532789" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>confidence</code> (0–1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d6-b788-f13646f8bfe3" class="">1.5 Recommendation package</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d5-a86c-d3ad11a50a27" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>rec_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a1-8ef7-cac1df044275" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d5-868e-d766899184bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>primary_failure_layer</code> (enum: <code>c1</code>…<code>c7</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f4-acdb-fc0421615365" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>secondary_risks</code> (array)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ce-8457-ff419a45fa4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>recommended_action_type</code> (enum: <code>message</code>, <code>structure</code>, <code>timing</code>, 
<code>no_go</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805d-964b-fc7c9daea7ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>recommended_actions</code> (array of Action)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8008-8333-c4b6ef2b58bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>predictions</code> (probabilities)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-8554-c1c8c82bb279" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>explainability</code> (required)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8004-9aa6-e66437b2dcbe" class="">Action object:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802e-8508-c70c57703725" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>action_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80be-be30-f8e11fb0ab89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>type</code> (enum: <code>message</code>, <code>structure</code>, <code>timing</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-9ad6-da1b3cf7c8ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>payload</code> (object; 
see below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ec-ac9c-dfd2cf3c5e16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>expected_delta</code> (object: deltas on C1–C7 + DCI)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c6-bbc1-fd4c0be69e47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>risk_tradeoffs</code> (string)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-806b-9b0a-d85ff67938c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e1-b05d-ef4e696e6013" class="">2) Endpoints</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80e8-9ad4-f2fe7b382ba9" class="">2.1 Create deal</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8028-8e7e-dddbf199d0e6" class=""><code>POST /deals</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800b-a23f-ea7ea5202301" class=""><strong>Request</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/components/prism-json.min.js" integrity="sha512-QXFMVAusM85vUYDaNgcYeU3rzSlc+bTV4JvkfJhjxSHlQEo+ig53BtnGkvFTiNJh8D+wv6uWAQ2vJaVmxe8d3w==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f3-8312-c005e6f15d4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;deal_type&quot;: &quot;mna&quot;,
  &quot;stage&quot;: &quot;negotiation&quot;,
  &quot;currency&quot;: &quot;USD&quot;,
  &quot;size&quot;: 25000000,
  &quot;reversibility&quot;: &quot;low&quot;,
  &quot;time_horizon_days&quot;: 120,
  &quot;context&quot;: {
    &quot;geo_regions&quot;: [&quot;SEA&quot;, &quot;US&quot;],
    &quot;war_exposure&quot;: &quot;medium&quot;,
    &quot;energy_volatility&quot;: &quot;high&quot;,
    &quot;regulatory_drift&quot;: &quot;medium&quot;,
    &quot;capital_conditions&quot;: &quot;tight&quot;
  },
  &quot;stakeholder_graph&quot;: {
    &quot;nodes&quot;: [],
    &quot;edges&quot;: []
  }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-a3cd-ebce215098f0" class=""><strong>Response (201)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fe-a393-d7ee37124762" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{ &quot;deal_id&quot;: &quot;d_123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000&quot; }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-a7ce-f448bb358dc3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8066-b5ba-c4c08fedb006" class="">2.2 Add actors</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8093-a389-c8787e21d9c3" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/actors</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80be-94c6-c7ca47a664ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;actors&quot;: [
    { &quot;type&quot;: &quot;individual&quot;, &quot;role&quot;: &quot;Founder&quot;, &quot;authority&quot;: &quot;decision&quot; },
    { &quot;type&quot;: &quot;organization&quot;, &quot;role&quot;: &quot;Legal Counsel&quot;, &quot;authority&quot;: &quot;veto&quot; }
  ]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c0-9006-f34841992d67" class=""><strong>Response</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d6-b1c4-e4d71e5ceb6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;actors&quot;: [
    { &quot;actor_id&quot;: &quot;a_...1&quot;, &quot;role&quot;: &quot;Founder&quot; },
    { &quot;actor_id&quot;: &quot;a_...2&quot;, &quot;role&quot;: &quot;Legal Counsel&quot; }
  ]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-aa2d-ece177196c87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f2-906b-df65b1c2396e" class="">2.3 Ingest interaction events</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b4-ac4f-eacc1d1b42d1" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/events</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8064-a524-deb23db1e219" class="">Use this for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807e-834b-c140aa9cbdf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">emails</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8013-9ce4-e8d6795112ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chat logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a3-9c30-f5e0bbe9abbf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">meeting notes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8076-a474-da1b3ad098c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transcripts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8093-ac95-dd042e370cde" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;events&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;channel&quot;: &quot;email&quot;,
      &quot;timestamp&quot;: &quot;2026-02-08T10:20:30Z&quot;,
      &quot;from_actor_id&quot;: &quot;a_...1&quot;,
      &quot;to_actor_ids&quot;: [&quot;a_...2&quot;],
      &quot;content&quot;: &quot;We should revisit the valuation later. Need more internal alignment.&quot;
    }
  ]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8036-ab12-d28e80f5cb34" class=""><strong>Response</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-90d9-f821fbb25428" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{ &quot;accepted&quot;: 1, &quot;event_ids&quot;: [&quot;e_...&quot;] }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8055-9890-df3548e2fe43"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8026-a405-f64e863e6d33" class="">2.4 Score now (deal + actors)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-803c-b26f-c56cca15ba2d" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/score</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8016-8902-f91a3c0d7e72" class=""><strong>Request</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-805a-8084-ec3749acf676" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;mode&quot;: &quot;auto&quot;,
  &quot;granularity&quot;: [&quot;deal&quot;, &quot;actor&quot;],
  &quot;options&quot;: {
    &quot;use_harmonic_mean&quot;: true,
    &quot;threat_weights&quot;: { &quot;c1&quot;: 0.4, &quot;c3&quot;: 0.4, &quot;c6&quot;: 0.2 }
  }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d1-b930-dd073a0280fd" class=""><strong>Response</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f4-a6f2-dd2b26478e43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;deal_snapshot&quot;: {
    &quot;snapshot_id&quot;: &quot;s_...&quot;,
    &quot;timestamp&quot;: &quot;2026-02-08T10:22:00Z&quot;,
    &quot;scores&quot;: {
      &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.52,
      &quot;c2_emotion&quot;: 0.60,
      &quot;c3_biology&quot;: 0.38,
      &quot;c4_cognition&quot;: 0.55,
      &quot;c5_behavior&quot;: 0.41,
      &quot;c6_structure&quot;: 0.62,
      &quot;c7_context&quot;: 0.70
    },
    &quot;coherence&quot;: 0.49,
    &quot;threat&quot;: 0.63,
    &quot;dci&quot;: 0.03,
    &quot;confidence&quot;: 0.72
  },
  &quot;actor_snapshots&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;actor_id&quot;: &quot;a_...1&quot;,
      &quot;scores&quot;: { &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.44, &quot;c3_biology&quot;: 0.35, &quot;c6_structure&quot;: 0.58 },
      &quot;coherence&quot;: 0.42,
      &quot;threat&quot;: 0.71,
      &quot;confidence&quot;: 0.68
    }
  ]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c3-8d39-ce3c2b541e02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8083-9842-fdb1ccd6b21e" class="">2.5 Get recommendations (playbook output)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8016-b23c-c5513db46135" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/recommend</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800e-9ef7-da8d9a496ae8" class=""><strong>Request</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a0-baa2-d8de370ab5e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;objective&quot;: &quot;increase_close_probability&quot;,
  &quot;constraints&quot;: {
    &quot;no_pressure_language&quot;: true,
    &quot;no_ego_injury&quot;: true,
    &quot;avoid_increasing_c3_load&quot;: true
  },
  &quot;n_actions&quot;: 3
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8022-8cd8-c11f115861a1" class=""><strong>Response</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-979e-da99e350145a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;rec_id&quot;: &quot;r_...&quot;,
  &quot;primary_failure_layer&quot;: &quot;c3&quot;,
  &quot;secondary_risks&quot;: [&quot;c1&quot;, &quot;c5&quot;],
  &quot;recommended_action_type&quot;: &quot;timing&quot;,
  &quot;recommended_actions&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;action_id&quot;: &quot;act_1&quot;,
      &quot;type&quot;: &quot;timing&quot;,
      &quot;payload&quot;: {
        &quot;recommendation&quot;: &quot;pause&quot;,
        &quot;duration_days&quot;: 10,
        &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;C3 overload likely; pushing increases identity threat&quot;
      },
      &quot;expected_delta&quot;: { &quot;c3_biology&quot;: 0.18, &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.07, &quot;dci&quot;: 0.02 }
    },
    {
      &quot;action_id&quot;: &quot;act_2&quot;,
      &quot;type&quot;: &quot;message&quot;,
      &quot;payload&quot;: {
        &quot;message_variants&quot;: [
          &quot;Mình tạm khóa quy trình 1 bước nhỏ trước để giảm ma sát nội bộ. Khi anh/chị thấy thuận, mình chốt lại mốc tiếp theo.&quot;
        ]
      },
      &quot;expected_delta&quot;: { &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.10, &quot;c2_emotion&quot;: 0.06, &quot;dci&quot;: 0.01 }
    }
  ],
  &quot;predictions&quot;: {
    &quot;p_close_30d&quot;: 0.41,
    &quot;p_close_if_actions_applied&quot;: 0.58,
    &quot;p_relationship_damage_if_push&quot;: 0.66
  },
  &quot;explainability&quot;: {
    &quot;why&quot;: [
      &quot;Signals indicate biological overload (delays + deferrals + shorter replies)&quot;,
      &quot;Pushing now raises ego risk; recommend timing + reversible step&quot;
    ],
    &quot;evidence&quot;: [&quot;e_...&quot;]
  }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-803a-b737-cfc0edbfcfe7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8092-b070-e50cd25bef02" class="">2.6 Counterfactual simulation (“what if”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8050-a87c-f2de33e1ced5" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/simulate</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-809d-9f88-e0aeb4311ea0" class=""><strong>Request</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8036-9cc6-d920a235105d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;actions&quot;: [
    { &quot;type&quot;: &quot;structure&quot;, &quot;payload&quot;: { &quot;split_commitment&quot;: true, &quot;step1_reversible&quot;: true } },
    { &quot;type&quot;: &quot;timing&quot;, &quot;payload&quot;: { &quot;pause_days&quot;: 7 } }
  ]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8028-b9db-ec1fd6a0ed0e" class=""><strong>Response</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8046-90dd-febb230b4300" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;baseline&quot;: { &quot;dci&quot;: 0.03, &quot;p_close_30d&quot;: 0.41 },
  &quot;scenario&quot;: { &quot;dci&quot;: 0.06, &quot;p_close_30d&quot;: 0.55 },
  &quot;deltas&quot;: { &quot;c3_biology&quot;: 0.12, &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.08, &quot;c5_behavior&quot;: 0.09 }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8078-b4fe-d960b0eff5a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8095-a5a0-e16ede34e56c" class="">2.7 Webhooks (real-time alerting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f0-9518-d1709d64f635" class=""><code>POST /webhooks</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8071-8878-c2c2a51a0f3c" class="">Events:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8094-b7d1-dd167af46d0f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deal.risk_spike</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8017-975f-e7c8e67e426a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>actor.threat_crossed</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c4-81e8-f9bd336e5971" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deal.no_go</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801f-8332-ec646e3d10c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deal.window_open</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8098-92af-c9447b77cd8a" class="">Payload includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8022-86a1-d6730d3ad459" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">layer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8037-8711-d2652de4924c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8072-af77-f88d9e877a11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">recommended minimal action</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8085-82f7-cab4d5d55186"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80a6-9669-f0802a61f332" class="">3) Error Handling</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8098-831f-d7ceaf70ea37" class="">Standard errors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-8137-d147655b821c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>400 invalid_request</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807f-b371-c37866fb64a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>401 unauthorized</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-959f-ebc47c5068ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>403 forbidden</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801b-b3d2-d9ecf697317a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>404 not_found</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-a1d9-dbefe29f97e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>409 conflict</code> (idempotency)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8010-85c5-d8bf19a36faa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>422 validation_error</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-8c15-ed61fae4a4d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>429 rate_limited</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8086-9483-ea1f09be01c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>500 internal_error</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-b8c9-e74cd8d36300" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b7-a9c2-e581c8234271" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" 
tyle="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{ &quot;error&quot;: { &quot;code&quot;: &quot;validation_error&quot;, &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Unknown deal_type&quot; } }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cc-a30c-c3f2938b87e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8013-a1b2-daccde74272e" class="">4) Security &amp; 
Compliance (API-level)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-801d-b61f-d1ce040526fb" class="">Required:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8050-a44e-ec6b236f14ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TLS 1.2+</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807e-a176-ed07da0a78b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Token rotation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-a193-f9001cda0b3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audit logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b4-b808-d2d2736ebc06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data retention policies per tenant</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-805b-b55c-dee0c896730d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PII minimization + redaction endpoint (optional)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-b016-e50f51b1fcc6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“No manipulation” constraints enforced server-side</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-808b-8e2f-fb6eed274bfa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8081-9296-c2f1d29b7ac1" class="">5) Minimal Scoring Interface (Manual Override)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c0-b9ab-c59e891049ac" class="">To support expert-in-the-loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ed-8a5f-fd424d6358eb" class=""><code>POST /deals/{deal_id}/score/manual</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-807d-8d8e-c592a34f6c7e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;actor_id&quot;: &quot;a_...1&quot;,
  &quot;scores&quot;: {
    &quot;c1_identity&quot;: 0.55,
    &quot;c3_biology&quot;: 0.40,
    &quot;c6_structure&quot;: 0.60
  },
  &quot;rationale&quot;: &quot;Founder under exhaustion; legal unclear on veto.&quot;
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f4-8334-edf237d863ed"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8035-97db-cad9ebb2b764" class="">6) Versioning</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8027-8505-d9f50f8e3b6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>v1</code> stable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8016-837f-cbcf774991f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Breaking changes → <code>v2</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802b-a9b2-d8d46c7572fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scoring formula versioned: <code>model_version</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c7-8dcf-c938a988bcf0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-808b-a7c1-d54bd7efd022" class="">If you want, 
next I can deliver one of these (tell me which):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-807e-84da-e59e20bc9abf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>OpenAPI 3.1 YAML</strong> (copy-paste into tooling)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c3-bb70-c7d4422d14ff" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>DB schema</strong> (Postgres tables)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="301c5e6f-95bd-809b-8bac-e3f8a611dffc" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Event taxonomy</strong> (exact list of features + weights for v1)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/components/prism-yaml.min.js" integrity="sha512-6O/PZimM3TD1NN3yrazePA4AbZrPcwt1QCGJrVY7WoHDJROZFc9TlBvIKMe+QfqgcslW4lQeBzNJEJvIMC8WhA==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><pre id="301c5e6f-95bd-8082-9e28-c29f75915c80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-yaml" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">openapi: 3.1.0
info:
  title: GPDE API
  version: &quot;1.0.0&quot;
  description: &gt;
    Global Predictive Deal Engine (GPDE) predicts deal coherence, threat, failure modes (C1–C7),
    and recommends interventions (message/structure/timing/no-go). Designed for sales, M&amp;A,
    diplomacy, and leadership. Outputs are explainable and constrained to threat-reduction.
servers:
  - url: https://api.gpde.ai/v1
security:
  - bearerAuth: []
tags:
  - name: Deals
  - name: Actors
  - name: Events
  - name: Scoring
  - name: Recommendations
  - name: Simulation
  - name: Webhooks

paths:
  /deals:
    post:
      tags: [Deals]
      summary: Create a deal container
      operationId: createDeal
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealCreateRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;201&quot;:
          description: Deal created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealCreateResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}:
    get:
      tags: [Deals]
      summary: Get deal details
      operationId: getDeal
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Deal details
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Deal&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

    patch:
      tags: [Deals]
      summary: Update deal metadata (partial)
      operationId: updateDeal
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealUpdateRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Deal updated
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Deal&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/actors:
    post:
      tags: [Actors]
      summary: Add one or more actors (stakeholders)
      operationId: addActors
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorAddRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Actors added
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorAddResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

    get:
      tags: [Actors]
      summary: List actors for a deal
      operationId: listActors
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Actor list
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorListResponse&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/events:
    post:
      tags: [Events]
      summary: Ingest interaction events (email/chat/call/meeting/doc)
      operationId: ingestEvents
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/EventIngestRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Events accepted
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/EventIngestResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/score:
    post:
      tags: [Scoring]
      summary: Compute scores (deal and/or actor granularity)
      operationId: scoreDeal
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Score snapshots created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/score/manual:
    post:
      tags: [Scoring]
      summary: Manual score override (expert-in-the-loop)
      operationId: manualScore
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ManualScoreRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Manual snapshot stored
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ManualScoreResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/recommend:
    post:
      tags: [Recommendations]
      summary: Generate recommendations (message/structure/timing/no-go)
      operationId: recommend
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/RecommendRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Recommendation package
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/RecommendResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /deals/{deal_id}/simulate:
    post:
      tags: [Simulation]
      summary: Run counterfactual simulation on proposed actions
      operationId: simulate
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/DealId&quot;
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/SimulateRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;200&quot;:
          description: Simulation results
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/SimulateResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;404&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/NotFound&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

  /webhooks:
    post:
      tags: [Webhooks]
      summary: Register a webhook endpoint
      operationId: createWebhook
      parameters:
        - $ref: &quot;#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey&quot;
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/WebhookCreateRequest&quot;
      responses:
        &quot;201&quot;:
          description: Webhook registered
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/WebhookCreateResponse&quot;
        &quot;400&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/BadRequest&quot;
        &quot;401&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/Unauthorized&quot;
        &quot;422&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/ValidationError&quot;
        &quot;429&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/RateLimited&quot;
        &quot;500&quot;:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/responses/InternalError&quot;

components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT

  parameters:
    DealId:
      name: deal_id
      in: path
      required: true
      schema:
        type: string
      description: Deal identifier (UUID string)
    IdempotencyKey:
      name: Idempotency-Key
      in: header
      required: false
      schema:
        type: string
      description: Idempotency key for write requests

  responses:
    BadRequest:
      description: Bad request
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;
    Unauthorized:
      description: Unauthorized
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;
    NotFound:
      description: Not found
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;
    ValidationError:
      description: Validation error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;
    RateLimited:
      description: Rate limited
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;
    InternalError:
      description: Internal server error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse&quot;

  schemas:
    DealType:
      type: string
      enum: [sales, mna, partnership, diplomacy, leadership]

    DealStage:
      type: string
      enum: [prospecting, discovery, proposal, negotiation, diligence, closing, post_close]

    Reversibility:
      type: string
      enum: [high, medium, low]

    CapitalConditions:
      type: string
      enum: [loose, neutral, tight]

    ExposureLevel:
      type: string
      enum: [low, medium, high]

    ActorType:
      type: string
      enum: [individual, organization, state]

    AuthorityType:
      type: string
      enum: [decision, veto, influencer, operator, unknown]

    AlignmentType:
      type: string
      enum: [supportive, neutral, resistant, unknown]

    ChannelType:
      type: string
      enum: [email, chat, call, meeting, doc]

    Granularity:
      type: string
      enum: [deal, actor]

    FailureLayer:
      type: string
      enum: [c1, c2, c3, c4, c5, c6, c7]

    RecommendedActionType:
      type: string
      enum: [message, structure, timing, no_go]

    ActionType:
      type: string
      enum: [message, structure, timing]

    ScoreValue:
      type: number
      minimum: 0
      maximum: 1

    ScoresC1C7:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [c1_identity, c2_emotion, c3_biology, c4_cognition, c5_behavior, c6_structure, c7_context]
      properties:
        c1_identity:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c2_emotion:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c3_biology:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c4_cognition:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c5_behavior:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c6_structure:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        c7_context:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;

    Context:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        geo_regions:
          type: array
          items:
            type: string
        war_exposure:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ExposureLevel&quot;
        energy_volatility:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ExposureLevel&quot;
        regulatory_drift:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ExposureLevel&quot;
        capital_conditions:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/CapitalConditions&quot;

    DealCreateRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [deal_type, stage, currency, reversibility, time_horizon_days]
      properties:
        deal_type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealType&quot;
        stage:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealStage&quot;
        currency:
          type: string
          description: ISO 4217 currency code
          minLength: 3
          maxLength: 3
        size:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        reversibility:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Reversibility&quot;
        time_horizon_days:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        context:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Context&quot;
        stakeholder_graph:
          type: object
          nullable: true
          description: Authority/veto/influence graph (implementation-defined)

    DealCreateResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [deal_id]
      properties:
        deal_id:
          type: string

    DealUpdateRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        stage:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealStage&quot;
        size:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        reversibility:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Reversibility&quot;
        time_horizon_days:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        context:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Context&quot;
        stakeholder_graph:
          type: object
          nullable: true

    Deal:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [deal_id, deal_type, stage, currency, reversibility, time_horizon_days]
      properties:
        deal_id:
          type: string
        deal_type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealType&quot;
        stage:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/DealStage&quot;
        currency:
          type: string
        size:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        reversibility:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Reversibility&quot;
        time_horizon_days:
          type: integer
        context:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Context&quot;
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    ActorCreate:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [type, role, authority]
      properties:
        type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorType&quot;
        role:
          type: string
        authority:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/AuthorityType&quot;
        alignment:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/AlignmentType&quot;
        notes:
          type: string
          nullable: true

    Actor:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actor_id, type, role, authority]
      properties:
        actor_id:
          type: string
        type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorType&quot;
        role:
          type: string
        authority:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/AuthorityType&quot;
        alignment:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/AlignmentType&quot;
        notes:
          type: string
          nullable: true
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    ActorAddRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actors]
      properties:
        actors:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActorCreate&quot;

    ActorAddResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actors]
      properties:
        actors:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Actor&quot;

    ActorListResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actors]
      properties:
        actors:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Actor&quot;

    Event:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [channel, timestamp, from_actor_id, to_actor_ids, content]
      properties:
        channel:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ChannelType&quot;
        timestamp:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        from_actor_id:
          type: string
        to_actor_ids:
          type: array
          items:
            type: string
        content:
          type: string
        metadata:
          type: object
          nullable: true

    EventIngestRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [events]
      properties:
        events:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Event&quot;

    EventIngestResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [accepted, event_ids]
      properties:
        accepted:
          type: integer
        event_ids:
          type: array
          items:
            type: string

    ThreatWeights:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        c1:
          type: number
          minimum: 0
        c3:
          type: number
          minimum: 0
        c6:
          type: number
          minimum: 0
      description: Threat weights for (C1 risk, C3 load, 1-C6)

    ScoreOptions:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        use_harmonic_mean:
          type: boolean
          default: true
        threat_weights:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ThreatWeights&quot;
        dci_mode:
          type: string
          enum: [product, harmonic]
          default: product

    ScoreRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [mode, granularity]
      properties:
        mode:
          type: string
          enum: [auto]
        granularity:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Granularity&quot;
        options:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreOptions&quot;

    ScoreSnapshot:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [snapshot_id, timestamp, scope, scores, coherence, threat, dci, confidence]
      properties:
        snapshot_id:
          type: string
        timestamp:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        scope:
          type: string
          enum: [deal, actor]
        actor_id:
          type: string
          nullable: true
        scores:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoresC1C7&quot;
        coherence:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        threat:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        dci:
          type: number
          minimum: 0
          maximum: 1
          description: Deal Coherence Index (configured by dci_mode)
        confidence:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;

    ScoreResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [deal_snapshot]
      properties:
        deal_snapshot:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreSnapshot&quot;
        actor_snapshots:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreSnapshot&quot;

    ManualScoreRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actor_id, scores]
      properties:
        actor_id:
          type: string
        scores:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          properties:
            c1_identity:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c2_emotion:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c3_biology:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c4_cognition:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c5_behavior:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c6_structure:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
            c7_context:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
          description: Provide any subset; server fills missing values from latest auto score.
        rationale:
          type: string
          nullable: true

    ManualScoreResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [snapshot]
      properties:
        snapshot:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreSnapshot&quot;

    RecommendConstraints:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        no_pressure_language:
          type: boolean
          default: true
        no_ego_injury:
          type: boolean
          default: true
        avoid_increasing_c3_load:
          type: boolean
          default: true

    RecommendRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [objective]
      properties:
        objective:
          type: string
          enum: [increase_close_probability, reduce_risk, stabilize_relationship]
        constraints:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/RecommendConstraints&quot;
        n_actions:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 10
          default: 3

    ActionPayloadMessage:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [message_variants]
      properties:
        message_variants:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            type: string

    ActionPayloadStructure:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        split_commitment:
          type: boolean
        step1_reversible:
          type: boolean
        cap_downside:
          type: boolean
        clarify_veto:
          type: boolean
        define_escalation_path:
          type: boolean

    ActionPayloadTiming:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        recommendation:
          type: string
          enum: [pause, proceed, wait_window]
        duration_days:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        window_description:
          type: string
          nullable: true

    ExpectedDelta:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      properties:
        c1_identity:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c2_emotion:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c3_biology:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c4_cognition:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c5_behavior:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c6_structure:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        c7_context:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1
        dci:
          type: number
          minimum: -1
          maximum: 1

    Action:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [action_id, type, expected_delta]
      properties:
        action_id:
          type: string
        type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActionType&quot;
        payload:
          oneOf:
            - $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActionPayloadMessage&quot;
            - $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActionPayloadStructure&quot;
            - $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActionPayloadTiming&quot;
        expected_delta:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ExpectedDelta&quot;
        risk_tradeoffs:
          type: string
          nullable: true

    Predictions:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [p_close_30d]
      properties:
        p_close_30d:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        p_close_if_actions_applied:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        p_relationship_damage_if_push:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;

    Explainability:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [why]
      properties:
        why:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            type: string
        evidence:
          type: array
          items:
            type: string

    RecommendResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [rec_id, primary_failure_layer, recommended_action_type, recommended_actions, predictions, explainability]
      properties:
        rec_id:
          type: string
        primary_failure_layer:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/FailureLayer&quot;
        secondary_risks:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/FailureLayer&quot;
        recommended_action_type:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/RecommendedActionType&quot;
        recommended_actions:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Action&quot;
        predictions:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Predictions&quot;
        explainability:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/Explainability&quot;

    SimulateRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [actions]
      properties:
        actions:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            type: object
            additionalProperties: false
            required: [type, payload]
            properties:
              type:
                $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ActionType&quot;
              payload:
                type: object

    SimulateResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [baseline, scenario, deltas]
      properties:
        baseline:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [dci, p_close_30d]
          properties:
            dci:
              type: number
              minimum: 0
              maximum: 1
            p_close_30d:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        scenario:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [dci, p_close_30d]
          properties:
            dci:
              type: number
              minimum: 0
              maximum: 1
            p_close_30d:
              $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ScoreValue&quot;
        deltas:
          $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/ExpectedDelta&quot;

    WebhookEventType:
      type: string
      enum: [deal.risk_spike, actor.threat_crossed, deal.no_go, deal.window_open]

    WebhookCreateRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [url, events]
      properties:
        url:
          type: string
          format: uri
        secret:
          type: string
          nullable: true
          description: Secret for signing webhook payloads
        events:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: &quot;#/components/schemas/WebhookEventType&quot;

    WebhookCreateResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [webhook_id]
      properties:
        webhook_id:
          type: string

    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [error]
      properties:
        error:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [code, message]
          properties:
            code:
              type: string
            message:
              type: string
            details:
              type: object
              nullable: true</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8020-acc4-d415f43c6d0c" class="">Below is the <strong>v1 Event Taxonomy</strong> for GPDE: <strong>exact feature list + default weights</strong> that map raw interaction events → C1–C7 scores → Threat/Coherence. 
This is implementable as-is.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-808b-ba8f-c256e42e8eef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b7-b45e-c862ecdf8818" class="">GPDE v1 Event Taxonomy</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8039-baac-d051b1945ef3" class=""><strong>Exact Features + Weights (v1 defaults)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-803f-ac08-e9971a321dcb" class="">A) Event object fields required (for feature extraction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80fd-988d-f7d431fe3ec5" class="">Each event yields a feature vector.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802c-b8c8-ef709c1a6c33" class=""><strong>Inputs (minimum)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-804c-812e-cf652d5a8cda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>channel</code> ∈ {email, chat, call, meeting, doc}</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8022-873d-e6d708477f3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802f-8b15-dedf19fe1348" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>from_actor_id</code>, 
<code>to_actor_ids</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b9-a2cf-e0bfbf204d4a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>content</code> (text or transcript)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8074-9d66-fed46486834f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metadata</code>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-807b-a888-c349b684c14e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>response_latency_sec</code> (if it’s a reply)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8019-8eca-e640e5b380f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>duration_sec</code> (call/meeting)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8056-ad7d-f9fc6ed3e747" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>participants_count</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8053-ba22-f3eef482b85a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>attachments_count</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-806b-a63a-e41b6957ffe3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>thread_depth</code> (email)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-809c-9cd7-e11cd50a38b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>message_length_chars</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-8806-d91e826081d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>sentiment_proxy</code> (optional; 
can be computed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b5-b4c8-db18299322c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>locale</code> (optional)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c1-8110-f82f1806e977"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8012-94f0-d2dfe752b966" class="">B) Feature families (MECE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c9-8163-e5b2ef68e854" class="">B1) Cadence &amp; Latency Features (time-series)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ce-9517-e35442555798" class="">Computed per actor and per deal with rolling windows (7d / 14d / 30d).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8050-a183-e4f5fbfdecc2" class=""><strong>F1. Response latency ratio (RLR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8014-97d1-d7b4394c7c19" class=""><code>RLR = current_latency / actor_baseline_latency</code> (clipped 0–5)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8099-904a-df43afe1c1ff" class=""><strong>F2. Silence gap (SG)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-809a-9e55-f07cd47c940d" class=""><code>SG = hours_since_last_response</code> (normalized vs baseline)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8070-9fe6-cf4425f4d269" class=""><strong>F3. Cadence volatility (CV)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8002-abe6-c7385ce2d60e" class="">Std dev of inter-message intervals / mean interval</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c9-aaec-e5aef6cad83d" class=""><strong>F4. 
Meeting density (MD)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8019-875c-edc63ecddbc2" class="">Meetings per 7d per actor (normalized)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8049-b08d-f940e0326a19" class=""><strong>F5. After-hours load (AHL)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cc-9826-fb65b7ec5d47" class="">% events outside 8:00–19:00 local</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d9-a835-e2926400b0a0" class=""><strong>Default layer weights (Cadence block)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b7-b7ae-e9f8bacc3ed4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C3 (Biology): <strong>0.40</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8034-a6af-d2867ebbe940" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C5 (Behavior friction): <strong>0.30</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-b8d2-e4e0285dd5f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C4 (Cognition): <strong>0.15</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80cc-84c8-e2b8f57247a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C2 (Emotion): <strong>0.10</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8096-90e7-df101bfb54f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C1 (Identity): <strong>0.05</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8096-8d29-d8caee98839e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-8064-bd7a-fc09527ed8f4" class="">B2) Commitment &amp; Execution Features (deal mechanics)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d5-a78c-dd0e29d05117" class=""><strong>F6. 
Commitment step count (CSC)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f9-a386-ea96948b9aa3" class="">of explicit commitments in message (dates, deliverables)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8094-9cb4-fe14f3a7ae4b" class=""><strong>F7. Commitment reversibility index (CRI)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80f0-9867-fbc71666df6d" class="">Detect “reversible step” language: pilot, trial, phase-1, non-binding</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b1-a0a7-c11537304881" class=""><strong>F8. Deadline certainty score (DCS)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8002-964b-d7f9b98d790f" class="">Explicit dates + ownership + next step clarity</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8007-b799-eeb624ddb8ac" class=""><strong>F9. Deliverable completion rate (DCR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800c-aed1-c1b132b823b3" class="">Delivered items / promised items in window</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80c4-bb9e-c2f6e9003b87" class=""><strong>F10. 
Scope creep rate (SCR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-803b-94d1-eec954df0d50" class="">New requirements introduced per interaction</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-808d-8bc9-d17d22afa58f" class=""><strong>Default layer weights (Commitment block)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d1-82b2-e535ba1cd88f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C5: <strong>0.35</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8093-89fe-f2ad61ab2267" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C6: <strong>0.25</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-80de-8a27-ca945b2c8404" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C4: <strong>0.20</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-801b-8531-ccd35bf2a3fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C1: <strong>0.10</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="301c5e6f-95bd-8068-8434-ebc6f4c6b55b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C3: <strong>0.10</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="301c5e6f-95bd-8025-8c8b-caeba59c9f55"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="301c5e6f-95bd-80d8-b335-f07a3998a6fb" class="">B3) Language: Threat, Control, Status, Face (identity &amp; ego)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-802a-9006-cbc136badb0a" class="">These are <strong>lexicon + pattern</strong> features. v1 uses deterministic rules; v2 can learn.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80aa-8bf6-f7b45eae5937" class=""><strong>F11. 
Authority assertion frequency (AAF)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80bd-866a-ec4b57698f0d" class="">Mentions of hierarchy, title, “final decision”, “approval”, “my call”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8040-aed8-c56b3b0c6ddc" class=""><strong>F12. Credit/authorship control (CAC)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8023-9e74-d9177530f90f" class="">“my idea”, “we did”, “I led”, “don’t present without me”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-8009-96bc-dc351a05e7e9" class=""><strong>F13. Face-protection markers (FPM)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-806a-aef1-daa5d32c1487" class="">Softeners: “just to clarify”, “not saying you did wrong”, “no offense”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80ba-a496-c4691745e858" class=""><strong>F14. Ego injury triggers detected (EITD)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-800e-9de6-df1daf54d9b0" class="">Direct criticism, blame, public exposure threats, “you failed”, “unacceptable”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="301c5e6f-95bd-80b6-8d5f-d61bdc5d5282" class=""><strong>F15.
